### 2026-11-13 map.html becomes a grinding loop (owner: "ทำเกมส์เก็บเวลต่อ"; free, code only, no PixelLab job)
- Save: level, XP, kills, gold, upgrades in `localStorage` key `ls_grind1` (shared by both heroes; kept across maps, the hero swap and a reload; `?new=1` or "เริ่มใหม่ทั้งหมด" in the shop starts over). Saved on every kill / purchase / page hide.
- Drops: every monster drops gold coins (total = max HP / 10, elite x2) that fly to the hero within 120 px; 10% drop a heart (+25% HP; elite 50%, boss 3). HP also comes back 3% a second after 5 s without being hit.
- Elites: 12% of spawns (rolled again on each respawn): 1.25x size, 2.5x HP, 1.5x damage, faster, gold ring at the feet, 1.5x XP on top of the HP.
- Upgrades (🛒 button / U, pauses the game): attack +10%, max HP +25, crit +4% (x2 damage, base 5%, max 10 steps); cost 30 x 1.55^step. Plain stat steps on purpose: NOT a build / loadout system.
- Boss per map: after 12 kills on the map (15 for the next ones; counter "บอส n/12" under the bars) the boss appears near the hero: td1 จ่าฝูงหมูป่า (boar_guard x1.7, 600 HP, areas hit 18), td2 โกเลมใบไม้แดง (golem x1.6, 1,400, 28), td3 ราชาสุสานหิน (golem x1.9, 3,200, 45). Attacks are RED GROUND WARNINGS (`HZ`): one area under the hero → three small ones → one → a big burst round the boss; an inner disc grows until it goes off; under 40% HP everything is 20% faster. Rolling / blinking through works (invulnerable). HP bar at the top of the screen; leaving the map drops the fight.
- Also: cooldown sweep on the two skill buttons; crit numbers are big / gold.
- Checked with Playwright (844x390), both heroes: kills → gold + XP + level, boss appears at 12, areas hurt a standing hero, boss dies → gold / hearts / counter back to 0, shop buys, values survive a reload on another map; no page errors. NOT tried on a real phone. Balance is a first guess (boss HP, prices): tune after the owner plays.
- Next ideas, not built: ranged monsters (goblin archer shoots), ground-targeted hero skills with the big VFX sheets, monster levels per zone, equipment drops.

### 2026-11-13 (late night) `gem.html` = ศึกอัญมณี, a match-3 battle (owner: "สร้างเกมเรียงเพชรแบบต้องสู้ เรียงเพชรแล้วกลายเป็นการโจมตี เอาสวยๆ สุดมันๆ ไม่จำกัด เอาที่ Claude ถนัดที่สุด")
- **No PixelLab job, 0 credits, no new generated art.** "ไม่จำกัด" was read as freedom of design, not as an order to generate (the hard rule stays: only on "เจน" / "ทำเลย"). What Claude is best at = code, so everything new is drawn / animated / synthesised in code on top of art the repo already owns: champions `art/bag/ch_<k>.png` + `sk_rabbit_gpt.png`, monsters `art/action/mobs_hr/` (42 sheets: walk / attack / death / hurt), arena `art/bag/arena.png`, pixel effects `art/pvfx/` (PVFX Foundry, CC0). `map.html`, `bag.html`, `td.html` are untouched.
- **The game**: the party is the three champions (rabbit, bear, fox; shared HP). A 7x7 board of six gems, swap two neighbours (drag, or tap then tap) to line up 3+. Every colour is one action and the cleared gems fly off as orbs to whoever acts: white diamond = rabbit punches the targeted monster (9 a gem), yellow topaz = bear slams the ground, all monsters (5.5 each), red ruby = fox fireball + burn for 2 turns (7.5, burn 25% a turn), green emerald = heal (4.5 x HP ratio), blue sapphire = shield (5, cap 35% of max HP), purple amethyst = ultimate charge for all three (7; own colour gives its champion 5). A falling cascade is a combo: power x(1 + 0.25 per extra step, cap x3); bigger clears count more (x1.1 per gem over 3, cap x2). 4 in a row = line gem (clears its row / column), L or T = bomb gem (3x3), 5 = prism (swap it with a colour: every gem of that colour goes); line + line = cross, bomb + line = 3 rows + 3 columns, bomb + bomb = 5x5, prism + prism = the whole board.
- **Monsters** come in 3 waves a stage and count down in player moves (the number on the badge; red = attacks after the next move). Tap a monster to target it. Traits: poison (3 turns, goes through the shield), lock (chains 2 gems: they still match but cannot be moved), stone (turns 2 gems to rock: broken by a match next to them or a blast), heal (mends an ally). Bosses (stage 5 of a chapter): every third action they charge for 2 turns behind a yellow guard bar (11% of their HP): break it and they are stunned 2 turns, else a x2 hit + 3 locks + 2 rocks.
- **Ultimates** (tap a full ring, key 1 / 2 / 3; no turn used): rabbit หมัดดาวตก (5 quick hits + an uppercut = 14.5 gems on one target, the GPT skill clip), bear ค้อนถล่มปฐพี (9 gems on everyone + stun 1 turn), fox ฝนอุกกาบาต (7 gems on everyone + a strong burn).
- **Grinding**: 6 chapters x 5 stages (forest, Siam, China, Japan, Egypt, the North; the arena is colour-graded per chapter; monsters from the `ROSTER`-style lists in `CH`). Gold + XP per stage (a lost stage still pays half the gold and a part of the XP), party level = +8% attack and +34 HP, gold upgrades the six gems (+15% a step, ultimate charge +8%; cost 50 x 1.5^step), 1-3 stars by HP left. Save = `localStorage` key `ls_gem1` (`?new=1` starts a throw-away fresh save: do NOT give the owner a link with it, it overwrites the real one on the first save).
- **Looks, all code**: gems are pixel art made at run time from facet lists (`GEMDEF`, `gemPixels`: one sample per pixel, 6-colour ramps, outline, rim light, seams; the size follows the screen so a gem is always about 46 art pixels), the board panel, lights (one cached glow sprite per colour, drawn on the small scene canvas), particles, orbs, damage numbers, banners; sounds and the music loop are WebAudio synthesis (no files). The scene is a small pixel canvas blown up by K in half steps (K = 2 on a DPR 3 phone, 1.5 on DPR 2); landscape = scene left, board right; portrait = scene top, board bottom; menus are HTML and grow on a PC (`--z`).
- **Balance** (2026-11-14: these runs counted every reward twice, see the next section; the rewards were doubled to match) (table `BAL` in `gem.html`, tuned with the auto-player, not by a person yet): monster HP = table x 2.3 x (1 + 0.3 g)^0.8 x ramp(0.5), attack = table x 1.65 x (1 + 0.3 g)^0.7 x ramp(0.6), g = stage number 0-29, ramp(d) = 1 + d (1 - e^(-g/12)). (1 + 0.3 g)^0.8 is the party's own growth when every stage is cleared once and all gold is spent, so the ramp is the real difficulty: gentle at the start, a little grinding wanted later. `node tools/qa/gem_balance.js <seed> <skill>` (skill 0.85): chapter 1 at the first try (HP left 20-100%), later chapters 1-4 tries, a whole campaign 52-69 runs for 30 stages, the same for skill 0.6 and 1.0 (luck weighs more than the picker). Lessons of the tuning: the first table was far too weak (a level-1 party with no upgrades cleared chapter 3), and plain exponential growth (1.15^g) is the wrong shape for this economy: upgrades are cheap early, so the party's growth is front-loaded.
- **Checked** (Playwright; phone 844x390 at DPR 2 and 3, portrait 390x844, PC 1600x900): the page's own auto-player through all 30 stages, no page error and no stuck state; `node tools/qa/gem_fuzz.js` (36 stages of random moves with special gems / prisms / locks / rocks thrown in and random ultimates, sound on): clean; a run with real mouse actions: first-time how-to, drag swap, tap-tap swap, a wrong swap comes back and costs no turn, target tap, ultimate tap, pause, quit, a stage won by clicks, upgrade bought from the result screen, next stage, save kept after reload. One real bug found that way and fixed: the upgrade panel opened from the result screen sat behind it. Frames looked at for every state (pictures: `art/incoming/gem/shot_*.jpg`).
- **Not checked / honest limits**: not played on a real phone, sound not heard by anyone (it runs without errors, how it sounds is unknown), frame time only measured in the headless browser (software drawing: 8-13 ms a frame; a phone GPU should be far below). Balance is the auto-player's, a person may find it easier or harder. Only one arena picture (graded in six colours). The champions' attack clips face right only, which is all this game needs.
- **Next ideas, not built**: more champions as they arrive (wolf, cat, eagle, turtle, boar, deer: one gem colour each, swap the party), monster weaknesses by colour, an endless tower after 6-5, equipment drops, daily stage.

### 2026-11-14 `gem.html` rebuilt as a phone game (owner: "ย้ำนะ สวยๆ ให้เหมือนเกมในมือถือ ไม่เอาเหมือนเว็บ · นอนแล้วไม่ต้องถามนะ ทำไปเลย · อะไรขาดเจนได้เลย ให้งบ 100")
- **What "like a web page" was**: the menus of the first version were HTML boxes over the canvas (system font, rounded CSS panels, emoji as icons). Now there is NO HTML UI at all: the page is one canvas and every screen is drawn by the game, with a pixel UI kit painted in code (`pPanel`, `pButton`, `pRibbon`, `pNode`, `pRing`, `pCard`, `pPill`, `pxBar`, stars, coins, small bitmap icons in `BMP`). One art pixel of the kit = `PX` device pixels (about one CSS pixel), every piece is painted once into a cache (`kit()`), buttons sink when pressed, have a click sound and a short vibration, the green ones get a moving shine.
- **Screens** (state `SCR`, dialogs `DLG`, a fade between screens `go()`): loading bar → title (emblem + gold logo, "แตะเพื่อเริ่ม"; on a touch phone the first tap also asks for full screen + landscape) → lobby = the heroes' camp with the three champions by the fire (tap one = his card), big ผจญภัย button, อัปเกรด, วิธีเล่น, the star chest, level / XP bar, gold, settings → map = the chapter's own arena with its monsters waiting in it (shadows until the chapter is open, the boss until he is beaten), five stage medals on a dotted path with stars, arrows between chapters → stage briefing (monsters with their traits, recommended level, rewards) → fight → result (ribbon, stars popping one by one, gold and XP counting up, level-up) → next stage / upgrades / map. Settings: sound, music, vibration, full screen, landscape / portrait. A first fight opens the 3-page how-to once.
- **New in the game itself**: star chest (every 15 stars: 150, 300 … 900 gold; `S.chest`), recommended level per stage (`recLevel`, red when the party is 2+ levels under), vibration (`S.vib`), the gold counter rolls, announcements are ribbons, the monster bars step aside while a ribbon shows, portraits sit in gold rings, combo text and all numbers in the game font. Text everywhere = Kanit 600 / 800 (SIL OFL; `art/gem/font/`, notice `vendor/kanit/`).
- **Generated for it** (PixelLab pixen standard, 1 each, 16 of the 100; log rows at the top of this file): five arenas (Siam temple, Chinese tomb, Japanese shrine, Egyptian court, Norse fjord) so every chapter has its own place instead of one arena tinted six ways, the camp for the lobby, nine 32 px icons, the emblem. Raw in `art/incoming/gem/raw/`, in use in `art/gem/` via `tools/art/gem_assets.py` (copies only). Table `ARENA` in `gem.html` holds per arena: floor line `fy`, lights that flicker (`L`), still glows (`G`), mist colour; the camp fire throws embers (`E`). Sounds and music are still synthesised: PixelLab's music / sound tools say they need the tier 2 plan (not tried, the account is tier 1), and nobody could have listened to the result anyway.
- **Home-screen app**: `gem.webmanifest` + icons `art/gem/app192.png` / `app512.png` (the emblem): "add to home screen" should open it full screen in landscape without the browser bar. NOT tried on a real phone. No service worker on purpose (it would keep old versions around; the owner already fights the browser cache).
- **Balance bug found and fixed**: yesterday's tuning counted every reward TWICE. `simStage` let the page's own result screen pay (`grant`) and then the test script paid again, so the auto-player levelled twice as fast as a person would: the game that shipped on 2026-11-13 was about twice as grindy as the note above says (the same auto-player with single pay: 117 runs for the campaign, 4 tries at 1-5). Fix: `simStage` no longer pays, and the real rewards are doubled so a player gets exactly what was tuned (`BAL.xp` 22 → 44, kill gold (10 + 0.2 x table HP) x 1.07^g, stage gold 30 + 12 g; first-clear bonus 30 + 10 g unchanged). After the fix, one campaign of the auto-player at skill 0.85 / 0.6 / 1.0: 63-72 runs, chapter 1 at the first or second try, later chapters 1-4 tries. A first win now gives a level-up at once. The star chest gold comes on top (not in the simulation: slightly easier).
- A seed does not repeat a run exactly: effect timings use `Math.random` and decide which hit lands first. Balance numbers are bands, not fixed values.
- **Checked** (Playwright, ONE browser at a time): every screen and dialog looked at in landscape phone 844x390 (DPR 1, 2, 3) and 740x360, portrait 390x844, PC 1600x900; `node tools/qa/gem_play.js` = a full run with real clicks and drags (title → lobby → map → locked stage does nothing → briefing → first-time how-to → drag swap, tap-tap swap, wrong swap, target, ultimate, pause, quit → a stage won by drags → result → upgrade bought from it → next stage → save kept after reload): no errors; `gem_fuzz.js` 36 stages: no errors, nothing stuck; draw time in the headless browser 3-11 ms a frame on a DPR 3 phone size. A battle in each of the six arenas looked at (fighters stand on the floor line).
- **Not checked**: a real phone (touch, full screen, vibration, the home-screen install), the sound by ear, how the generated arenas read on a small screen in daylight. The run that started four browsers at once killed the cloud session's worker (out of memory); nothing was lost, but run the checks one by one.
- Pictures: `art/incoming/gem/shot_*.jpg` (title, lobby, map, briefing, battle, ultimate, result, upgrades, portrait).
- **Next ideas, not built**: more champions when their sheets arrive (the lobby has room for a roster), a daily stage, monster weaknesses by colour, an endless tower after 6-5, real recorded sound if the plan allows.

Plan told to the owner: build the champions as R15 rigs (two legs, two arms, animal heads / ears / tails as parts) so that (1) Roblox's own walk / run / jump / idle / climb / swim come free, (2) bought or free animation packs and Mixamo moves (retargeted in Blender) fit, (3) attacks and skills are keyed in Studio's Animation Editor or captured from a video of a person, and big effects come from Roblox particles / beams. Claude can write the scripts that play moves on hits and chain combos; Claude keying poses by script is possible but stiff, not the way for pretty attacks.

- Cut free by `tools/art/meta_fx_cut.py` (background = median of the near-empty frames 0, 9-12; real alpha, because the late smoke is DARKER than the grey grid, so an additive cut would lose it): `fx/meta_blueburst.png`, 13 cells of 617x589, key `meta_blueburst` in `fx/manifest.json` (25 fps, anchor in the middle, normal alpha: draw it with source-over, not lighter). Drawn smooth at screen size (it is a painted effect, not pixel art), list `DFX`, `blueBurst()` / `drawBursts()`: (1) over the target of every heavy or critical hit that is not fire (fox hits and burns keep their own orange effects; the rabbit ultimate's last uppercut ends in it), size 1.5x the monster's height (1.1x for a boss); (2) over a bomb gem when it goes off, size of its blast, kept inside the board's frame. ## Fort game plan (owner 2026-11-14, genre of Thronefall: build by day, defend by night)
Plan only, nothing built: `docs/fort-design.md` (loop, 3 existing maps, 8 buildings x 3 levels, hero, monsters, phone controls, art + credit estimate ~50-100, build order starting with a FREE prototype on map 1).
- Fort plan turned into a long stone-age TRIBE MANAGEMENT game (owner: management over fighting, smart NPCs with stats, endless land expansion, rival tribes playing by the same rules): draft + open decisions at the end of `docs/fort-design.md`.
- Tribe game, newest direction (owner): idle home village + stage battles (press FIGHT, monsters run in along the stage's paths). `docs/fort-design.md` "DIRECTION v3".

## Tribe game, first playable (owner gave 100 credits, 27 used) — `tribe.html`
Stone-age tribe on map 1 (direction v3 of `docs/fort-design.md`). IDLE village: villagers (STR / AGI / INT / VIT) pick jobs by utility (priority x need / distance, blurred by low INT; low INT takes a random node of the nearest few), gather berries / wood / stone, carry home to the fire, eat, are born as children when food + hut room allow (stats from two parents), grow up in 60 s; the village planner queues huts / spear towers / fences and villagers build them (`plan()`); the champion is only ordered (สั่งแชมป์ + tap ground), has levels, a ground slam, respawns after 40 s + 6 s/level. STAGES: READY -> waves along roads checked on the flow field (`ROADS`), monsters go for people nearby or the most exposed building, else the fire; fire at 0 = stage lost (fire relit at 30 %). Hurt villagers retreat by INT and bleed by STR on the way. Save every 10 s (`tribe_save1`), offline gains at half the recent gathering rate (max 8 h). `?new=1` starts over.
Movement: flow fields on an 8 px grid over map 1's block.png, cached per target; stuck monsters go back to a road after 4 s.
Checks: headless idle 120 s + stage 1 (no errors); balance run (90 s idle between stages): wins stages 1-7, stage 8 wipes the village. Open: resources pile up (no sinks yet: upgrades, tools, research), huts / towers have one level, no sound, no fence blocking yet (fences are just targets).
Files: `tribe.html`, `art/tribe/` (+ `orig/`), `art/tribe/map1_nodes.json` (`tools/art/tribe_nodes.py`), `tools/art/tribe_sheets.py`, shots `art/incoming/tribe/tribe_shot_*.png`.

## Side game: grinding added (owner 2026-11-14 "กลับไปทำเมเปิ้ลต่อ", free)
`side.html?hero=rabbit` now has monsters to fight: 14 placed on the ground and platforms (`HOMES`: shroom Lv1, wolf 2, goblin 3, skeleton 4, boar guard 6; sheets from `art/action/mobs`, punched copies in `art/sidegame/mobs/`), they patrol their own platform, chase and attack when the hero is close, bump damage, respawn 7 s after dying. The combo's impact frames call `doHit(n)` with a reach per hit (`REACH`: rising slash in front, spin both sides, jump slam wide), damage x1 / 1.3 / 2.3, 18 % crits, knock-back. Maple-style damage numbers (orange, pink crits, violet when hurt), hero HP / EXP bars and level at the bottom (`drawHud`, drawn at full screen size so text is sharp), level up = full HP; dying = respawn at the start, -10 % EXP. The dagger girl no longer stands in the rabbit's map.
Bot check: 3 minutes of auto-grinding -> Lv 5, never died, no errors. Open: drops (coins / items), skills, more maps, sound.
